 1491:     def _changed_files(self, worktree: Path) -> list[str]:
 1492:         tracked = git(worktree, "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD", "--").stdout.splitlines()
 1493:         untracked = git(worktree, "ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard").stdout.splitlines()
 1494:         return sorted({normalize_path(p) for p in tracked + untracked if p.strip()})
 1495: 
 1496:     def _scope_check(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], label: str, run_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
 1497:         # Revalidate after every builder/test boundary. A builder can replace a
 1498:         # previously safe file with a symlink before staging.
 1499:         ensure_paths_confined(worktree, allowed)
 1500:         # Collect changed files BEFORE cache cleanup so any out-of-scope files are detected
 1501:         changed_before_cleanup = self._changed_files(worktree)
 1502:         outside_before = sorted(set(changed_before_cleanup) - set(allowed))
 1503:         if outside_before:
 1504:             # Check if any outside files are legitimate builder cache artifacts
 1505:             known_cache_patterns = {".aider.tags.cache.v4", ".gemini", ".agy"}
 1506:             rogue_files = [
 1507:                 p for p in outside_before
 1508:                 if not any(p == cp or p.startswith(cp + "/") for cp in known_cache_patterns)
 1509:             ]
 1510:             if rogue_files:
 1511:                 raise FactoryError(f"scope violation after {label}: {rogue_files}", "scope")
 1512: 
 1513:         clean_builder_generated_artifacts(worktree)
 1514:         changed = self._changed_files(worktree)
 1515:         outside = sorted(set(changed) - set(allowed))
 1516:         evidence = {"label": label, "changed_files": changed, "allowed_files": allowed, "outside_scope": outside}
 1517:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"scope_{label}.json", evidence)
 1518:         if outside:
 1519:             raise FactoryError(f"scope violation after {label}: {outside}", "scope")
 1520:         return changed
 1521: 
 1522:     def _detect_test(self, worktree: Path) -> str | None:
 1523:         package = worktree / "package.json"
 1524:         if package.is_file():
 1525:             try:
 1526:                 data = read_json(package)
 1527:                 if isinstance(data.get("scripts", {}).get("test"), str):
 1528:                     return "npm test"
 1529:             except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
 1530:                 pass
 1531:         if (worktree / "pytest.ini").is_file() or (worktree / "pyproject.toml").is_file() or (worktree / "tests").is_dir():
 1532:             return "pytest"
 1533:         if (worktree / "Cargo.toml").is_file():
 1534:             return "cargo test"
 1535:         if (worktree / "go.mod").is_file():
 1536:             return "go test ./..."
 1537:         return None
 1538: 
 1539:     def _test(self, worktree: Path, config: Config, run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1540:         test_command = config.test_command or self._detect_test(worktree)
 1541:         if not test_command:
 1542:             result = {"sequence": sequence, "command": None, "returncode": None, "passed": False, "error": "no test command supplied or detected"}
 1543:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.json", result)
 1544:             raise FactoryError("no test command supplied or safely auto-detected", "test")
 1545:         test_command = normalize_test_command(test_command)
 1546:         test_env = os.environ.copy()
 1547:         # Test runners must not manufacture Git-visible Python bytecode outside
 1548:         # the approved file boundary. Other generated outputs remain subject to
 1549:         # the normal post-test scope gate and should be listed in .gitignore.
 1550:         test_env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
 1551:         lib_dir = worktree / "lib"
 1552:         if lib_dir.is_dir():
 1553:             current_pp = test_env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")
 1554:             test_env["PYTHONPATH"] = f"{str(lib_dir)}{os.pathsep}{current_pp}" if current_pp else str(lib_dir)
 1555:         executed = command(
 1556:             test_command,
 1557:             cwd=worktree,
 1558:             env=test_env,
 1559:             timeout=config.test_timeout,
 1560:             shell=True,
 1561:         )
 1562:         log_path = run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.log"
 1563:         write_text(log_path, (executed.stdout or "") + ("\n" + executed.stderr if executed.stderr else ""))
 1564:         result = {"sequence": sequence, "command": test_command, "returncode": executed.returncode, "passed": executed.returncode == 0, "log": str(log_path)}
 1565:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.json", result)
 1566:         return result

 1575:             + "\n".join(f"- {p}" for p in plan["allowed_files"])

 1634:     def _stage(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> tuple[str, str, list[str]]:
 1635:         git(worktree, "reset", "--quiet")
 1636:         changed = self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"prestage_{sequence}", run_dir)
 1637:         if not changed:
 1638:             raise FactoryError("builder produced no changes", "builder")
 1639:         git(worktree, "add", "--", *allowed)
 1640:         diff_text = git(worktree, "diff", "--cached", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff").stdout
 1641:         if not diff_text:
 1642:             raise FactoryError("staged candidate diff is empty", "git")
 1643:         digest = hashlib.sha256(diff_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
 1644:         write_text(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.diff", diff_text)
 1645:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.json", {"sha256": digest, "changed_files": changed})
 1646:         return diff_text, digest, changed

 1648:     def _candidate_source_context(
 1649:         self,
 1650:         worktree: Path,
 1651:         allowed: list[str],
 1652:         changed: list[str],
 1653:         run_dir: Path,
 1654:         sequence: int,
 1655:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1656:         """Build a bounded, read-only source snapshot for Sol's review.
 1657: 
 1658:         The index is used instead of the working tree so the reviewer sees the
 1659:         exact candidate that will be hash-checked, including newly created or
 1660:         deleted files. Planner and Luna transcripts are deliberately omitted.
 1661:         """
 1662:         remaining = 80_000
 1663:         files: dict[str, Any] = {}
 1664:         for relative in sorted(set(allowed) | set(changed)):
 1665:             shown = command(["git", "show", f":{relative}"], cwd=worktree, timeout=30)
 1666:             if shown.returncode != 0:
 1667:                 files[relative] = {"state": "deleted-or-absent"}
 1668:                 continue
 1669:             raw = shown.stdout
 1670:             if "\x00" in raw:
 1671:                 files[relative] = {"state": "binary-or-non-text", "included": False}
 1672:                 continue
 1673:             excerpt = raw[: min(remaining, 12_000)]
 1674:             files[relative] = {
 1675:                 "state": "present",
 1676:                 "content": excerpt,
 1677:                 "truncated": len(excerpt) < len(raw),
 1678:             }
 1679:             remaining -= len(excerpt)
 1680:             if remaining <= 0:
 1681:                 break
 1682:         context = {
 1683:             "source": "staged index exact candidate state",
 1684:             "base_commit": git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip(),
 1685:             "files": files,
 1686:             "note": "Read-only context; omitted files were not included or exceeded the context limit.",
 1687:         }
 1688:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_source_context_{sequence}.json", context)
 1689:         return context
 1690: 
 1691:     def _review(
 1692:         self,
 1693:         *,
 1694:         run_dir: Path,
 1695:         config: Config,
 1696:         plan: dict[str, Any],
 1697:         diff_text: str,
 1698:         digest: str,
 1699:         changed: list[str],
 1700:         candidate_source_context: dict[str, Any],
 1701:         tests: list[dict[str, Any]],
 1702:         sequence: int,
 1703:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1704:         evidence_packet = make_review_evidence_packet(
 1705:             diff_text=diff_text,
 1706:             digest=digest,
 1707:             changed=changed,
 1708:             candidate_source_context=candidate_source_context,
 1709:             tests=tests,
 1710:             plan=plan,
 1711:             sequence=sequence,
 1712:         )
 1713:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"evidence_packet_review_{sequence}.json", evidence_packet)
 1714:         # Deliberately omit planner transcript/history. This is a fresh process and clean input.
 1715:         payload = {
 1716:             "original_request": config.request,
 1717:             "sol_plan": plan,
 1718:             "evidence_packet": evidence_packet,
 1719:             "candidate_diff": {
 1720:                 "sha256": digest,
 1721:                 "changed_files": changed,
 1722:                 "patch": diff_text,
 1723:             },
 1724:             "candidate_source_context": candidate_source_context,
 1725:             "test_results": tests,
 1726:         }
 1727:         prompt = (
 1728:             "Goal:\nIndependently decide whether the exact candidate diff is safe and complete.\n\n"
 1729:             "Context:\nYou are Sol Reviewer in a completely fresh context. Review the request, plan, exact staged diff, "
 1730:             "the read-only source snapshot reconstructed from the staged candidate index, and test results. "
 1731:             "The source snapshot is bounded and may mark large/binary files as omitted.\n\n"
 1732:             "Constraints:\n- Do not edit files or call tools.\n- Do not rely on planner conversation history.\n"
 1733:             "- Check acceptance criteria, correctness, regressions, architecture, and security.\n"
 1734:             "- Do not reveal hidden reasoning.\n\n"
 1735:             "Output:\nReturn only strict JSON with exactly: decision (APPROVE or REJECT), reviewed_diff_sha256 "
 1736:             "(copy the supplied hash exactly), criteria (array of objects), findings (string array), "
 1737:             "required_changes (string array), route (none, builder, or luna).\n\n"
 1738:             "Done when:\nAPPROVE means route=none and no required_changes. REJECT means concrete changes and route=builder or luna.\n\n"
 1739:             "Input:\n" + json.dumps(payload, indent=2)
 1740:         )
 1741:         review = self._role_call(
 1742:             run_dir=run_dir,
 1743:             role="Sol Reviewer",
 1744:             provider=config.reviewer_provider or config.sol_provider,
 1745:             model=config.reviewer_model or config.sol_model,
 1746:             prompt=prompt,
 1747:             label=f"sol_review_{sequence}",
 1748:             timeout=config.role_timeout,
 1749:             reasoning=config.reviewer_reasoning,
 1750:         )
 1751:         review = validate_review(review, digest)
 1752:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"sol_review_{sequence}.json", review)
 1753:         return review
 1754: 
 1755:     def _create_checkpoint(self, worktree: Path, run_dir: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1756:         """Capture a safe checkpoint of the worktree state with durable refs and binary fidelity."""
 1757:         # 1. Capture stash commit if any changes exist
 1758:         stash_res = command(["git", "stash", "create"], cwd=worktree)
 1759:         if stash_res.returncode != 0:
 1760:             raise FactoryError(f"git stash create failed: {stash_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1761:         stash_commit = stash_res.stdout.strip()
 1762:         stash_ref = None
 1763:         if stash_commit:
 1764:             stash_ref = f"refs/factory/checkpoints/{label}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
 1765:             ref_res = command(["git", "update-ref", stash_ref, stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1766:             if ref_res.returncode != 0:
 1767:                 raise FactoryError(f"git update-ref failed: {ref_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1768: 
 1769:         # 2. Capture diffs with returncode validation
 1770:         unstaged_res = command(["git", "diff"], cwd=worktree)
 1771:         if unstaged_res.returncode != 0:
 1772:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff failed: {unstaged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1773:         staged_res = command(["git", "diff", "--cached"], cwd=worktree)
 1774:         if staged_res.returncode != 0:
 1775:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff --cached failed: {staged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1776: 
 1777:         # 3. Capture untracked files safely with NUL delimiter and base64 encoding
 1778:         untracked_res = command(["git", "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--exclude-standard"], cwd=worktree)
 1779:         if untracked_res.returncode != 0:
 1780:             raise FactoryError(f"git ls-files failed: {untracked_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1781: 
 1782:         raw_paths = untracked_res.stdout.split("\0")
 1783:         untracked_files: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
 1784:         for rel_p in raw_paths:
 1785:             if not rel_p:
 1786:                 continue
 1787:             full_p = worktree / rel_p
 1788:             if full_p.is_symlink():
 1789:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1790:                     "type": "symlink",
 1791:                     "target": os.readlink(full_p),
 1792:                 }
 1793:             elif full_p.is_file():
 1794:                 content_bytes = full_p.read_bytes()
 1795:                 mode = full_p.stat().st_mode
 1796:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1797:                     "type": "file",
 1798:                     "data": base64.b64encode(content_bytes).decode("ascii"),
 1799:                     "mode": oct(mode),
 1800:                 }
 1801: 
 1802:         checkpoint = {
 1803:             "label": label,
 1804:             "created_at": now_iso(),
 1805:             "stash_commit": stash_commit,
 1806:             "stash_ref": stash_ref,
 1807:             "unstaged_diff": unstaged_res.stdout,
 1808:             "staged_diff": staged_res.stdout,
 1809:             "untracked_files": untracked_files,
 1810:         }
 1811:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"checkpoint_{label}.json", checkpoint)
 1812:         return checkpoint
 1813: 
 1814:     def _safe_rollback(self, worktree: Path, checkpoint: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path) -> None:
 1815:         """Safely restore the worktree to the state recorded in checkpoint and verify exact restoration."""
 1816:         # 1. Reset and clean strictly
 1817:         reset_res = command(["git", "reset", "--hard", "HEAD"], cwd=worktree)
 1818:         if reset_res.returncode != 0:
 1819:             raise FactoryError(f"git reset --hard failed: {reset_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1820:         clean_res = command(["git", "clean", "-fd", "-q"], cwd=worktree)
 1821:         if clean_res.returncode != 0:
 1822:             raise FactoryError(f"git clean failed: {clean_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1823: 
 1824:         # 2. Restore tracked changes
 1825:         stash_commit = checkpoint.get("stash_commit")
 1826:         if stash_commit:
 1827:             apply_res = command(["git", "stash", "apply", "--index", "--quiet", stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1828:             if apply_res.returncode != 0:
 1829:                 raise FactoryError(f"git stash apply --index failed: {apply_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1830:         else:
 1831:             staged_diff = checkpoint.get("staged_diff") or ""
 1832:             if staged_diff:
 1833:                 apply_staged = command(["git", "apply", "--index", "--whitespace=nowarn"], cwd=worktree, stdin=staged_diff)
 1834:                 if apply_staged.returncode != 0:
 1835:                     raise FactoryError(f"git apply --index failed: {apply_staged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1836:             unstaged_diff = checkpoint.get("unstaged_diff") or ""
 1837:             if unstaged_diff:

 1881:         checkpoint_untracked = checkpoint.get("untracked_files") or {}

 1883:             raise FactoryError(

 1892:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: symlink mismatch for {rel_p}", "rollback")
 1893:             elif expected_meta.get("type") == "file":
 1894:                 if not current_p.is_file():
 1895:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: {rel_p} is not a regular file", "rollback")

 1906:     def _pre_review_gate(
 1907:         self,
 1908:         worktree: Path,
 1909:         allowed: list[str],
 1910:         changed: list[str],
 1911:         diff_text: str,
 1912:         tests: list[dict[str, Any]],
 1913:         run_dir: Path,
 1914:         sequence: int,
 1915:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1916:         """Deterministic pre-review gate prior to calling Sol Reviewer."""
 1917:         gate_checks: dict[str, bool] = {}
 1918: 
 1919:         # 1. Tests must have passed before Sol Reviewer is invoked
 1920:         tests_ok = bool(tests and tests[-1].get("passed", False))
 1921:         gate_checks["tests_passed"] = tests_ok
 1922:         if not tests_ok:
 1923:             failure_info = {
 1924:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1925:                 "reason": "latest test run did not pass",
 1926:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1927:             }
 1928:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1929:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: tests have not passed", "pre_review_gate")
 1930: 
 1931:         # 2. Diff must be non-empty and scope strictly confined
 1932:         diff_ok = bool(diff_text.strip())
 1933:         scope_ok = bool(changed and set(changed).issubset(set(allowed)))
 1934:         gate_checks["diff_non_empty"] = diff_ok
 1935:         gate_checks["scope_confined"] = scope_ok
 1936:         if not diff_ok or not scope_ok:
 1937:             failure_info = {
 1938:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1939:                 "reason": "empty diff or out-of-scope files",
 1940:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1941:             }
 1942:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1943:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: invalid diff or scope", "pre_review_gate")
 1944: 
 1945:         # 3. No git conflict markers in candidate diff
 1946:         conflict_pattern = re.compile(r"^\+(<<<<<<<|=======|>>>>>>>)", re.MULTILINE)
 1947:         has_conflicts = bool(conflict_pattern.search(diff_text))
 1948:         gate_checks["no_conflict_markers"] = not has_conflicts
 1949:         if has_conflicts:
 1950:             failure_info = {
 1951:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1952:                 "reason": "merge conflict markers found in candidate diff",
 1953:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1954:             }
 1955:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1956:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: merge conflict markers detected", "pre_review_gate")
 1957: 
 1958:         # 4. Check syntax for Python files
 1959:         for rel_path in changed:
 1960:             if rel_path.endswith(".py"):
 1961:                 file_path = worktree / rel_path
 1962:                 if file_path.is_file():
 1963:                     try:
 1964:                         content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 1965:                         compile(content, str(file_path), "exec")
 1966:                     except SyntaxError as exc:
 1967:                         gate_checks["syntax_valid"] = False
 1968:                         failure_info = {
 1969:                             "gate_passed": False,
 1970:                             "reason": f"syntax error in {rel_path}: {exc}",
 1971:                             "checks": gate_checks,
 1972:                         }
 1973:                         atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1974:                         raise FactoryError(f"deterministic pre-review gate failed: syntax error in {rel_path}: {exc}", "pre_review_gate")
 1975:         gate_checks["syntax_valid"] = True
 1976: 
 1977:         gate_record = {
 1978:             "gate_passed": True,
 1979:             "sequence": sequence,
 1980:             "checks": gate_checks,
 1981:             "checked_at": now_iso(),
 1982:         }
 1983:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", gate_record)
 1984:         return gate_record
 1985:

 2494:                             evidence={"returncode": test_result["returncode"], "log": test_result["log"]},
 2495:                         )
 2496:                         ledger.record_event(
 2497:                             phase="test",
 2498:                             action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
 2499:                             result="failed",
 2500:                             details=test_result,
 2501:                             issue_links=[test_issue],
 2502:                             artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
 2503:                         )
 2504:                 if not test_result["passed"]:
 2505:                     raise FactoryError("tests failed after reviewer rework and Luna repair budget", "test")
 2506:                 review_sequence += 1
 2507:             raise FactoryError("review loop ended without approval", "review")
 2508:         except FactoryError as exc:
 2509:             phase = "blocked" if exc.category in {"preflight", "provider", "base"} else "failed"
 2510:             ledger.record_event(
 2511:                 phase=phase,
 2512:                 action="execution_aborted",
 2513:                 result=exc.category,
 2514:                 details={"error": str(exc), "category": exc.category},
 2515:             )
 2516:             self._save(run_dir, state, phase, error=str(exc), error_category=exc.category, test_results=tests)
 2517:             raise
 2518: 
 2519:     def status(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 2520:         _, state, _ = self._load(identifier)
 2521:         return state
 2522: 
 2523:     def resume(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 2524:         run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 2525:         if state["phase"] == "committed":
 2526:             return state
 2527:         if state["phase"] not in {"planned", "blocked", "failed", "worktree_ready"}:
 2528:             raise FactoryError(f"run cannot be resumed safely from phase {state['phase']!r}", "state")
 2529:         if not (run_dir / "sol_plan.json").is_file():
 2530:             repo = self._repo(config.repo)
 2531:             try:
 2532:                 self._perform_planning(run_dir, state, config, repo)
 2533:             except FactoryError as exc:
 2534:                 phase = "blocked" if exc.category in {"preflight", "provider"} else "failed"
 2535:                 self._save(run_dir, state, phase, error=str(exc), error_category=exc.category)
 2536:                 raise
 2537:         else:
 2538:             state["warm_start"] = True
 2539:             atomic_json(run_dir / "warm_start.json", {"warm_start": True, "resumed_from": state.get("phase"), "timestamp": now_iso()})
 2540:             atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
 2541:         return self.execute(identifier)

 1143:         if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+", identifier):
 1144:             raise FactoryError("invalid run id", "input")

 1148:         state.update(updates)

 1153:     def _load(self, identifier: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any], Config]:
 1154:         run_dir = self._run_dir(identifier)
 1155:         state_path = run_dir / "run_state.json"
 1156:         if not state_path.is_file():

 1161:     def _repo(self, raw: str) -> Path:
 1162:         repo = Path(raw).expanduser().resolve()
 1163:         probe = command(["git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], cwd=repo, timeout=30)
 1164:         if probe.returncode != 0 or Path(probe.stdout.strip()).resolve() != repo:
 1165:             raise FactoryError(f"repository must be its Git top-level directory: {repo}", "input")
 1166:         return repo

 1169:         argv = ["status", "--porcelain=v1", "--untracked-files=all"]
 1170:         if paths is not None:
 1171:             argv.extend(["--", *paths])

 1174:     def _assert_source_unchanged(

 1176:     ) -> None:

 1212:                 paths = set()
 1213:                 for line in status_text.splitlines():

 1217:                         path_part = line[2:].strip()

 1219:                             path_part = path_part.split(" -> ")[-1].strip()

 1238:         tracked = git(repo, "ls-files").stdout.splitlines()

 1474:     def _create_worktree(self, run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], config: Config) -> Path:
 1475:         repo = Path(config.repo)
 1476:         current = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()
 1477:         if current != state["base_commit"]:
 1478:             raise FactoryError(
 1479:                 f"base moved since planning: expected {state['base_commit']}, found {current}", "base"
 1480:             )
 1481:         worktree = run_dir / "worktree"
 1482:         if worktree.is_dir():
 1483:             return worktree
 1484:         branch = f"hermes/task-{state['run_id']}"
 1485:         result = command(["git", "worktree", "add", "-b", branch, str(worktree), state["base_commit"]], cwd=repo, timeout=120)
 1486:         if result.returncode != 0:
 1487:             raise FactoryError(f"could not create isolated worktree: {(result.stderr or result.stdout).strip()}", "git")
 1488:         self._save(run_dir, state, "worktree_ready", branch=branch, worktree=str(worktree))
 1489:         return worktree
 1490: 
 1491:     def _changed_files(self, worktree: Path) -> list[str]:
 1492:         tracked = git(worktree, "diff", "--name-only", "HEAD", "--").stdout.splitlines()
 1493:         untracked = git(worktree, "ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard").stdout.splitlines()
 1494:         return sorted({normalize_path(p) for p in tracked + untracked if p.strip()})
 1495: 
 1496:     def _scope_check(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], label: str, run_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
 1497:         # Revalidate after every builder/test boundary. A builder can replace a
 1498:         # previously safe file with a symlink before staging.
 1499:         ensure_paths_confined(worktree, allowed)
 1500:         # Collect changed files BEFORE cache cleanup so any out-of-scope files are detected
 1501:         changed_before_cleanup = self._changed_files(worktree)
 1502:         outside_before = sorted(set(changed_before_cleanup) - set(allowed))
 1503:         if outside_before:
 1504:             # Check if any outside files are legitimate builder cache artifacts
 1505:             known_cache_patterns = {".aider.tags.cache.v4", ".gemini", ".agy"}
 1506:             rogue_files = [
 1507:                 p for p in outside_before
 1508:                 if not any(p == cp or p.startswith(cp + "/") for cp in known_cache_patterns)
 1509:             ]
 1510:             if rogue_files:
 1511:                 raise FactoryError(f"scope violation after {label}: {rogue_files}", "scope")
 1512: 
 1513:         clean_builder_generated_artifacts(worktree)
 1514:         changed = self._changed_files(worktree)
 1515:         outside = sorted(set(changed) - set(allowed))
 1516:         evidence = {"label": label, "changed_files": changed, "allowed_files": allowed, "outside_scope": outside}
 1517:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"scope_{label}.json", evidence)
 1518:         if outside:
 1519:             raise FactoryError(f"scope violation after {label}: {outside}", "scope")
 1520:         return changed

 1522:     def _detect_test(self, worktree: Path) -> str | None:
 1523:         package = worktree / "package.json"
 1524:         if package.is_file():
 1525:             try:
 1526:                 data = read_json(package)
 1527:                 if isinstance(data.get("scripts", {}).get("test"), str):
 1528:                     return "npm test"
 1529:             except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
 1530:                 pass
 1531:         if (worktree / "pytest.ini").is_file() or (worktree / "pyproject.toml").is_file() or (worktree / "tests").is_dir():
 1532:             return "pytest"
 1533:         if (worktree / "Cargo.toml").is_file():
 1534:             return "cargo test"
 1535:         if (worktree / "go.mod").is_file():
 1536:             return "go test ./..."
 1537:         return None

 1539:     def _test(self, worktree: Path, config: Config, run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1540:         test_command = config.test_command or self._detect_test(worktree)
 1541:         if not test_command:
 1542:             result = {"sequence": sequence, "command": None, "returncode": None, "passed": False, "error": "no test command supplied or detected"}
 1543:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.json", result)
 1544:             raise FactoryError("no test command supplied or safely auto-detected", "test")
 1545:         test_command = normalize_test_command(test_command)
 1546:         test_env = os.environ.copy()
 1547:         # Test runners must not manufacture Git-visible Python bytecode outside
 1548:         # the approved file boundary. Other generated outputs remain subject to
 1549:         # the normal post-test scope gate and should be listed in .gitignore.
 1550:         test_env["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
 1551:         lib_dir = worktree / "lib"
 1552:         if lib_dir.is_dir():
 1553:             current_pp = test_env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")
 1554:             test_env["PYTHONPATH"] = f"{str(lib_dir)}{os.pathsep}{current_pp}" if current_pp else str(lib_dir)
 1555:         executed = command(
 1556:             test_command,
 1557:             cwd=worktree,
 1558:             env=test_env,
 1559:             timeout=config.test_timeout,
 1560:             shell=True,
 1561:         )
 1562:         log_path = run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.log"
 1563:         write_text(log_path, (executed.stdout or "") + ("\n" + executed.stderr if executed.stderr else ""))
 1564:         result = {"sequence": sequence, "command": test_command, "returncode": executed.returncode, "passed": executed.returncode == 0, "log": str(log_path)}
 1565:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.json", result)
 1566:         return result

 1634:     def _stage(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> tuple[str, str, list[str]]:
 1635:         git(worktree, "reset", "--quiet")
 1636:         changed = self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"prestage_{sequence}", run_dir)
 1637:         if not changed:
 1638:             raise FactoryError("builder produced no changes", "builder")
 1639:         git(worktree, "add", "--", *allowed)
 1640:         diff_text = git(worktree, "diff", "--cached", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff").stdout
 1641:         if not diff_text:
 1642:             raise FactoryError("staged candidate diff is empty", "git")
 1643:         digest = hashlib.sha256(diff_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
 1644:         write_text(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.diff", diff_text)
 1645:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.json", {"sha256": digest, "changed_files": changed})
 1646:         return diff_text, digest, changed

 1648:     def _candidate_source_context(
 1649:         self,
 1650:         worktree: Path,
 1651:         allowed: list[str],
 1652:         changed: list[str],
 1653:         run_dir: Path,
 1654:         sequence: int,
 1655:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1656:         """Build a bounded, read-only source snapshot for Sol's review.
 1657: 
 1658:         The index is used instead of the working tree so the reviewer sees the
 1659:         exact candidate that will be hash-checked, including newly created or
 1660:         deleted files. Planner and Luna transcripts are deliberately omitted.
 1661:         """
 1662:         remaining = 80_000
 1663:         files: dict[str, Any] = {}
 1664:         for relative in sorted(set(allowed) | set(changed)):
 1665:             shown = command(["git", "show", f":{relative}"], cwd=worktree, timeout=30)
 1666:             if shown.returncode != 0:
 1667:                 files[relative] = {"state": "deleted-or-absent"}
 1668:                 continue
 1669:             raw = shown.stdout
 1670:             if "\x00" in raw:
 1671:                 files[relative] = {"state": "binary-or-non-text", "included": False}
 1672:                 continue
 1673:             excerpt = raw[: min(remaining, 12_000)]
 1674:             files[relative] = {
 1675:                 "state": "present",
 1676:                 "content": excerpt,
 1677:                 "truncated": len(excerpt) < len(raw),
 1678:             }
 1679:             remaining -= len(excerpt)
 1680:             if remaining <= 0:
 1681:                 break
 1682:         context = {
 1683:             "source": "staged index exact candidate state",
 1684:             "base_commit": git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip(),
 1685:             "files": files,
 1686:             "note": "Read-only context; omitted files were not included or exceeded the context limit.",
 1687:         }
 1688:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_source_context_{sequence}.json", context)
 1689:         return context
 1690: 
 1691:     def _review(
 1692:         self,
 1693:         *,

 1695:         config: Config,
 1696:         plan: dict[str, Any],
 1697:         diff_text: str,

 1704:         evidence_packet = make_review_evidence_packet(

 1707:             changed=changed,
 1708:             candidate_source_context=candidate_source_context,
 1709:             tests=tests,
 1710:             plan=plan,
 1711:             sequence=sequence,
 1712:         )
 1713:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"evidence_packet_review_{sequence}.json", evidence_packet)
 1714:         # Deliberately omit planner transcript/history. This is a fresh process and clean input.
 1715:         payload = {
 1716:             "original_request": config.request,
 1717:             "sol_plan": plan,
 1718:             "evidence_packet": evidence_packet,
 1719:             "candidate_diff": {
 1720:                 "sha256": digest,
 1721:                 "changed_files": changed,
 1722:                 "patch": diff_text,
 1723:             },
 1724:             "candidate_source_context": candidate_source_context,
 1725:             "test_results": tests,
 1726:         }
 1727:         prompt = (
 1728:             "Goal:\nIndependently decide whether the exact candidate diff is safe and complete.\n\n"
 1729:             "Context:\nYou are Sol Reviewer in a completely fresh context. Review the request, plan, exact staged diff, "
 1730:             "the read-only source snapshot reconstructed from the staged candidate index, and test results. "
 1731:             "The source snapshot is bounded and may mark large/binary files as omitted.\n\n"
 1732:             "Constraints:\n- Do not edit files or call tools.\n- Do not rely on planner conversation history.\n"
 1733:             "- Check acceptance criteria, correctness, regressions, architecture, and security.\n"
 1734:             "- Do not reveal hidden reasoning.\n\n"
 1735:             "Output:\nReturn only strict JSON with exactly: decision (APPROVE or REJECT), reviewed_diff_sha256 "
=== 1745-1845 ===
 1745:             model=config.reviewer_model or config.sol_model,
 1746:             prompt=prompt,
 1747:             label=f"sol_review_{sequence}",
 1748:             timeout=config.role_timeout,
 1749:             reasoning=config.reviewer_reasoning,
 1750:         )
 1751:         review = validate_review(review, digest)
 1752:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"sol_review_{sequence}.json", review)
 1753:         return review

 1755:     def _create_checkpoint(self, worktree: Path, run_dir: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1756:         """Capture a safe checkpoint of the worktree state with durable refs and binary fidelity."""
 1757:         # 1. Capture stash commit if any changes exist
 1758:         stash_res = command(["git", "stash", "create"], cwd=worktree)
 1759:         if stash_res.returncode != 0:
 1760:             raise FactoryError(f"git stash create failed: {stash_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1761:         stash_commit = stash_res.stdout.strip()
 1762:         stash_ref = None
 1763:         if stash_commit:
 1764:             stash_ref = f"refs/factory/checkpoints/{label}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
 1765:             ref_res = command(["git", "update-ref", stash_ref, stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1766:             if ref_res.returncode != 0:
 1767:                 raise FactoryError(f"git update-ref failed: {ref_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1768: 
 1769:         # 2. Capture diffs with returncode validation
 1770:         unstaged_res = command(["git", "diff"], cwd=worktree)
 1771:         if unstaged_res.returncode != 0:
 1772:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff failed: {unstaged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1773:         staged_res = command(["git", "diff", "--cached"], cwd=worktree)
 1774:         if staged_res.returncode != 0:
 1775:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff --cached failed: {staged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1776: 
 1777:         # 3. Capture untracked files safely with NUL delimiter and base64 encoding
 1778:         untracked_res = command(["git", "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--exclude-standard"], cwd=worktree)
 1779:         if untracked_res.returncode != 0:
 1780:             raise FactoryError(f"git ls-files failed: {untracked_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1781: 
 1782:         raw_paths = untracked_res.stdout.split("\0")
 1783:         untracked_files: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
 1784:         for rel_p in raw_paths:
 1785:             if not rel_p:
 1786:                 continue
 1787:             full_p = worktree / rel_p
 1788:             if full_p.is_symlink():
 1789:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1790:                     "type": "symlink",
 1791:                     "target": os.readlink(full_p),
 1792:                 }
 1793:             elif full_p.is_file():
 1794:                 content_bytes = full_p.read_bytes()
 1795:                 mode = full_p.stat().st_mode
 1796:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1797:                     "type": "file",
 1798:                     "data": base64.b64encode(content_bytes).decode("ascii"),
 1799:                     "mode": oct(mode),
 1800:                 }
 1801: 
 1802:         checkpoint = {
 1803:             "label": label,
 1804:             "created_at": now_iso(),
 1805:             "stash_commit": stash_commit,
 1806:             "stash_ref": stash_ref,
 1807:             "unstaged_diff": unstaged_res.stdout,
 1808:             "staged_diff": staged_res.stdout,
 1809:             "untracked_files": untracked_files,
 1810:         }
 1811:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"checkpoint_{label}.json", checkpoint)
 1812:         return checkpoint
 1813: 
 1814:     def _safe_rollback(self, worktree: Path, checkpoint: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path) -> None:
 1815:         """Safely restore the worktree to the state recorded in checkpoint and verify exact restoration."""
 1816:         # 1. Reset and clean strictly
 1817:         reset_res = command(["git", "reset", "--hard", "HEAD"], cwd=worktree)
 1818:         if reset_res.returncode != 0:
 1819:             raise FactoryError(f"git reset --hard failed: {reset_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1820:         clean_res = command(["git", "clean", "-fd", "-q"], cwd=worktree)
 1821:         if clean_res.returncode != 0:
 1822:             raise FactoryError(f"git clean failed: {clean_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1823: 
 1824:         # 2. Restore tracked changes
 1825:         stash_commit = checkpoint.get("stash_commit")
 1826:         if stash_commit:
 1827:             apply_res = command(["git", "stash", "apply", "--index", "--quiet", stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1828:             if apply_res.returncode != 0:
 1829:                 raise FactoryError(f"git stash apply --index failed: {apply_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1830:         else:
 1831:             staged_diff = checkpoint.get("staged_diff") or ""
 1832:             if staged_diff:
 1833:                 apply_staged = command(["git", "apply", "--index", "--whitespace=nowarn"], cwd=worktree, stdin=staged_diff)
 1834:                 if apply_staged.returncode != 0:
 1835:                     raise FactoryError(f"git apply --index failed: {apply_staged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1836:             unstaged_diff = checkpoint.get("unstaged_diff") or ""
 1837:             if unstaged_diff:
 1838:                 apply_unstaged = command(["git", "apply", "--whitespace=nowarn"], cwd=worktree, stdin=unstaged_diff)
 1839:                 if apply_unstaged.returncode != 0:
 1840:                     raise FactoryError(f"git apply unstaged failed: {apply_unstaged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1841: 
 1842:         # 3. Restore untracked files byte-for-byte and symlinks
 1843:         untracked_files = checkpoint.get("untracked_files") or {}
 1844:         for rel_p, file_meta in untracked_files.items():
 1845:             target_path = worktree / rel_p
=== 1885-2075 ===
 1885:                 "rollback",
 1886:             )
 1887:         for rel_p in post_raw_paths:
 1888:             expected_meta = checkpoint_untracked[rel_p]
 1889:             current_p = worktree / rel_p
 1890:             if expected_meta.get("type") == "symlink":
 1891:                 if not current_p.is_symlink() or os.readlink(current_p) != expected_meta.get("target"):
 1892:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: symlink mismatch for {rel_p}", "rollback")
 1893:             elif expected_meta.get("type") == "file":
 1894:                 if not current_p.is_file():
 1895:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: {rel_p} is not a regular file", "rollback")
 1896:                 curr_bytes = current_p.read_bytes()
 1897:                 curr_b64 = base64.b64encode(curr_bytes).decode("ascii")
 1898:                 if curr_b64 != expected_meta.get("data"):
 1899:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: content mismatch for {rel_p}", "rollback")
 1900: 
 1901:         atomic_json(
 1902:             run_dir / "last_rollback.json",
 1903:             {"restored_to": checkpoint.get("label"), "timestamp": now_iso()},
 1904:         )

 1906:     def _pre_review_gate(
 1907:         self,
 1908:         worktree: Path,
 1909:         allowed: list[str],
 1910:         changed: list[str],
 1911:         diff_text: str,
 1912:         tests: list[dict[str, Any]],
 1913:         run_dir: Path,
 1914:         sequence: int,
 1915:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1916:         """Deterministic pre-review gate prior to calling Sol Reviewer."""
 1917:         gate_checks: dict[str, bool] = {}
 1918: 
 1919:         # 1. Tests must have passed before Sol Reviewer is invoked
 1920:         tests_ok = bool(tests and tests[-1].get("passed", False))
 1921:         gate_checks["tests_passed"] = tests_ok
 1922:         if not tests_ok:
 1923:             failure_info = {
 1924:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1925:                 "reason": "latest test run did not pass",
 1926:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1927:             }
 1928:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1929:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: tests have not passed", "pre_review_gate")
 1930: 
 1931:         # 2. Diff must be non-empty and scope strictly confined
 1932:         diff_ok = bool(diff_text.strip())
 1933:         scope_ok = bool(changed and set(changed).issubset(set(allowed)))
 1934:         gate_checks["diff_non_empty"] = diff_ok
 1935:         gate_checks["scope_confined"] = scope_ok
 1936:         if not diff_ok or not scope_ok:
 1937:             failure_info = {
 1938:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1939:                 "reason": "empty diff or out-of-scope files",
 1940:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1941:             }
 1942:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1943:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: invalid diff or scope", "pre_review_gate")
 1944: 
 1945:         # 3. No git conflict markers in candidate diff
 1946:         conflict_pattern = re.compile(r"^\+(<<<<<<<|=======|>>>>>>>)", re.MULTILINE)
 1947:         has_conflicts = bool(conflict_pattern.search(diff_text))
 1948:         gate_checks["no_conflict_markers"] = not has_conflicts
 1949:         if has_conflicts:
 1950:             failure_info = {
 1951:                 "gate_passed": False,
 1952:                 "reason": "merge conflict markers found in candidate diff",
 1953:                 "checks": gate_checks,
 1954:             }
 1955:             atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1956:             raise FactoryError("deterministic pre-review gate failed: merge conflict markers detected", "pre_review_gate")
 1957: 
 1958:         # 4. Check syntax for Python files
 1959:         for rel_path in changed:
 1960:             if rel_path.endswith(".py"):
 1961:                 file_path = worktree / rel_path
 1962:                 if file_path.is_file():
 1963:                     try:
 1964:                         content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 1965:                         compile(content, str(file_path), "exec")
 1966:                     except SyntaxError as exc:
 1967:                         gate_checks["syntax_valid"] = False
 1968:                         failure_info = {
 1969:                             "gate_passed": False,
 1970:                             "reason": f"syntax error in {rel_path}: {exc}",
 1971:                             "checks": gate_checks,
 1972:                         }
 1973:                         atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", failure_info)
 1974:                         raise FactoryError(f"deterministic pre-review gate failed: syntax error in {rel_path}: {exc}", "pre_review_gate")
 1975:         gate_checks["syntax_valid"] = True
 1976: 
 1977:         gate_record = {
 1978:             "gate_passed": True,
 1979:             "sequence": sequence,
 1980:             "checks": gate_checks,
 1981:             "checked_at": now_iso(),
 1982:         }
 1983:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"pre_review_gate_{sequence}.json", gate_record)
 1984:         return gate_record
 1985: 
 1986:     def execute(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1987:         run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
 1988:         if state["phase"] == "committed":
 1989:             return state
 1990:         if not (run_dir / "sol_plan.json").is_file():
 1991:             raise FactoryError("run has no valid Sol plan; create a new run", "state")
 1992:         plan = read_json(run_dir / "sol_plan.json")
 1993:         allowed = plan["allowed_files"]
 1994:         ensure_paths_confined(Path(config.repo), allowed)
 1995:         tests: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
 1996:         checkpoints: list[str] = []
 1997: 
 1998:         root_obj_class = state.get("objective_class") or classify_objective(config.request)["classification"]
 1999:         ledger = MemoryLedger(run_dir, identifier, config.repo, root_obj_class)
 2000: 
 2001:         try:
 2002:             self._save(run_dir, state, "preflight_execution", error=None, error_category=None)
 2003:             # All three capabilities are proven before creating or changing the task worktree.
 2004:             self._capability_preflight(run_dir, state, config)
 2005:             ledger.record_event(phase="preflight_execution", action="capability_preflight", result="success")
 2006:             self._assert_source_unchanged(Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, "before_worktree")
 2007:             worktree = self._create_worktree(run_dir, state, config)
 2008:             # Record clean baseline of source checkout at worktree creation time
 2009:             state["source_status_before"] = self._source_status(Path(config.repo))
 2010:             ledger.record_event(
 2011:                 phase="worktree",
 2012:                 action="create_worktree",
 2013:                 result="success",
 2014:                 details={"branch": state.get("branch")},
 2015:             )
 2016:             clean_builder_generated_artifacts(worktree)
 2017:             ensure_paths_confined(worktree, allowed)
 2018:             self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, "before_builder", run_dir)
 2019:             cp_initial = self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, "initial")
 2020:             checkpoints.append("initial")
 2021:             ledger.record_event(phase="checkpoint", action="checkpoint_initial", result="success")
 2022:             self._save(run_dir, state, "building", checkpoints=checkpoints)
 2023:             self.builder.run(
 2024:                 worktree=worktree,
 2025:                 instruction=self._builder_instruction(config, plan),
 2026:                 allowed_files=allowed,
 2027:                 config=config,
 2028:                 run_dir=run_dir,
 2029:                 label="builder_initial",
 2030:             )
 2031:             ledger.record_event(
 2032:                 phase="build",
 2033:                 action="builder_initial",
 2034:                 result="success",
 2035:                 objective_class=root_obj_class,
 2036:                 artifacts=["builder_initial.log"],
 2037:             )
 2038:             self._assert_source_unchanged(Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, "after_builder_initial")
 2039:             self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, "after_builder", run_dir)
 2040: 
 2041:             test_sequence = 1
 2042:             test_result = self._test(worktree, config, run_dir, test_sequence)
 2043:             tests.append(test_result)
 2044:             self._assert_source_unchanged(Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, "after_test_1")
 2045:             self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_test_{test_sequence}", run_dir)
 2046:             if test_result["passed"]:
 2047:                 self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
 2048:                 checkpoints.append(f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
 2049:                 ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
 2050:                     "Test Runner",
 2051:                     resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
 2052:                 )
 2053:                 ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
 2054:                     "Luna Debugger",
 2055:                     resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
 2056:                 )
 2057:                 ledger.record_event(
 2058:                     phase="test",
 2059:                     action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
 2060:                     result="success",
 2061:                     details=test_result,
 2062:                     artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
 2063:                 )

  452: def atomic_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
  453:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
  454:     fd, temporary = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
  455:     try:
  456:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
  457:             json.dump(value, handle, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
  458:             handle.write("\n")
  459:             handle.flush()
  460:             os.fsync(handle.fileno())
  461:         os.replace(temporary, path)
  462:         os.chmod(path, 0o600)
  463:     finally:
  464:         if os.path.exists(temporary):
  465:             os.unlink(temporary)
  466: 
  467: 
  468: def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
  469:     with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
  470:         return json.load(handle)
  471: 
  472: 
  473: def write_text(path: Path, value: str) -> None:
  474:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
  475:     path.write_text(value, encoding="utf-8")
  476:     os.chmod(path, 0o600)

  605: def string_list(value: Any, field: str, *, allow_empty: bool = False) -> list[str]:
  606:     if not isinstance(value, list) or (not value and not allow_empty):
  607:         raise FactoryError(f"{field} must be a non-empty JSON array", "contract")
  608:     if not all(isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in value):
  609:         raise FactoryError(f"{field} must contain only non-empty strings", "contract")
  610:     return [item.strip() for item in value]

  613: def validate_plan(value: dict[str, Any], requested_files: list[str] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  614:     require_keys(
  615:         value,
  616:         {
  617:             "objective",
  618:             "allowed_files",
  619:             "implementation_steps",
  620:             "acceptance_criteria",
  621:             "recommended_tests",
  622:             "risks",
  623:         },

  625:     )

  627:         raise FactoryError("Sol plan objective must be a non-empty string", "contract")
  628:     value["allowed_files"] = normalized_paths(string_list(value["allowed_files"], "allowed_files"))
  629:     for key in ("implementation_steps", "acceptance_criteria", "recommended_tests"):
  630:         value[key] = string_list(value[key], key)
  631:     value["risks"] = string_list(value["risks"], "risks", allow_empty=True)

  641: def validate_luna(value: dict[str, Any], allowed_files: list[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  642:     require_keys(value, {"root_cause", "repair_steps", "files", "tests_to_rerun"}, "Luna diagnosis")
  643:     if not isinstance(value["root_cause"], str) or not value["root_cause"].strip():
  644:         raise FactoryError("Luna root_cause must be a non-empty string", "contract")
  645:     value["repair_steps"] = string_list(value["repair_steps"], "repair_steps")
  646:     value["files"] = normalized_paths(string_list(value["files"], "files"))
  647:     value["tests_to_rerun"] = string_list(value["tests_to_rerun"], "tests_to_rerun")
  648:     outside = sorted(set(value["files"]) - set(allowed_files))
  649:     if outside:

  654: def validate_review(value: dict[str, Any], expected_hash: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  655:     require_keys(
  656:         value,
  657:         {"decision", "reviewed_diff_sha256", "criteria", "findings", "required_changes", "route"},
  658:         "Sol review",
  659:     )
  660:     decision = value["decision"]
  661:     if decision not in {"APPROVE", "REJECT"}:
  662:         raise FactoryError("Sol review decision must be APPROVE or REJECT", "contract")
  663:     if value["reviewed_diff_sha256"] != expected_hash:
  664:         raise FactoryError("Sol reviewed hash does not match the staged candidate", "hash")
  665:     if not isinstance(value["criteria"], list) or not all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in value["criteria"]):
  666:         raise FactoryError("Sol review criteria must be an array of objects", "contract")
  667:     value["findings"] = string_list(value["findings"], "findings", allow_empty=True)
  668:     value["required_changes"] = string_list(value["required_changes"], "required_changes", allow_empty=True)
  669:     if value["route"] not in {"none", "builder", "luna"}:
  670:         raise FactoryError("Sol review route must be none, builder, or luna", "contract")
  671:     if decision == "APPROVE" and (value["route"] != "none" or value["required_changes"]):
  672:         raise FactoryError("APPROVE must use route=none with no required_changes", "contract")
  673:     if decision == "REJECT" and (value["route"] == "none" or not value["required_changes"]):
  674:         raise FactoryError("REJECT requires changes and a builder/luna route", "contract")
  675:     return value

 1522:     def _detect_test(self, worktree: Path) -> str | None:
 1523:         package = worktree / "package.json"
 1524:         if package.is_file():
 1525:             try:
 1526:                 data = read_json(package)
 1527:                 if isinstance(data.get("scripts", {}).get("test"), str):
 1528:                     return "npm test"
 1529:             except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
 1530:                 pass
 1531:         if (worktree / "pytest.ini").is_file() or (worktree / "pyproject.toml").is_file() or (worktree / "tests").is_dir():
 1532:             return "pytest"
 1533:         if (worktree / "Cargo.toml").is_file():
 1534:             return "cargo test"
 1535:         if (worktree / "go.mod").is_file():
 1536:             return "go test ./..."
 1537:         return None

 1539:     def _test(self, worktree: Path, config: Config, run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1540:         test_command = config.test_command or self._detect_test(worktree)
 1541:         if not test_command:

 1556:             test_command,
 1557:             cwd=worktree,
 1558:             env=test_env,
 1559:             timeout=config.test_timeout,
 1560:             shell=True,
 1561:         )
 1562:         log_path = run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.log"
 1563:         write_text(log_path, (executed.stdout or "") + ("\n" + executed.stderr if executed.stderr else ""))
 1564:         result = {"sequence": sequence, "command": test_command, "returncode": executed.returncode, "passed": executed.returncode == 0, "log": str(log_path)}
 1565:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"test_{sequence}.json", result)
 1566:         return result

 1568:     def _builder_instruction(self, config: Config, plan: dict[str, Any]) -> str:

 1634:     def _stage(self, worktree: Path, allowed: list[str], run_dir: Path, sequence: int) -> tuple[str, str, list[str]]:
 1635:         git(worktree, "reset", "--quiet")
 1636:         changed = self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"prestage_{sequence}", run_dir)
 1637:         if not changed:
 1638:             raise FactoryError("builder produced no changes", "builder")
 1639:         git(worktree, "add", "--", *allowed)
 1640:         diff_text = git(worktree, "diff", "--cached", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff").stdout
 1641:         if not diff_text:
 1642:             raise FactoryError("staged candidate diff is empty", "git")
 1643:         digest = hashlib.sha256(diff_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
 1644:         write_text(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.diff", diff_text)
 1645:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_{sequence}.json", {"sha256": digest, "changed_files": changed})
 1646:         return diff_text, digest, changed
 1647: 
 1648:     def _candidate_source_context(
 1649:         self,
 1650:         worktree: Path,
 1651:         allowed: list[str],
 1652:         changed: list[str],
 1653:         run_dir: Path,
 1654:         sequence: int,
 1655:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1656:         """Build a bounded, read-only source snapshot for Sol's review.
 1657: 
 1658:         The index is used instead of the working tree so the reviewer sees the
 1659:         exact candidate that will be hash-checked, including newly created or
 1660:         deleted files. Planner and Luna transcripts are deliberately omitted.
 1661:         """
 1662:         remaining = 80_000
 1663:         files: dict[str, Any] = {}
 1664:         for relative in sorted(set(allowed) | set(changed)):
 1665:             shown = command(["git", "show", f":{relative}"], cwd=worktree, timeout=30)
 1666:             if shown.returncode != 0:
 1667:                 files[relative] = {"state": "deleted-or-absent"}
 1668:                 continue
 1669:             raw = shown.stdout
 1670:             if "\x00" in raw:
 1671:                 files[relative] = {"state": "binary-or-non-text", "included": False}
 1672:                 continue
 1673:             excerpt = raw[: min(remaining, 12_000)]
 1674:             files[relative] = {
 1675:                 "state": "present",
 1676:                 "content": excerpt,
 1677:                 "truncated": len(excerpt) < len(raw),
 1678:             }
 1679:             remaining -= len(excerpt)
 1680:             if remaining <= 0:
 1681:                 break
 1682:         context = {
 1683:             "source": "staged index exact candidate state",
 1684:             "base_commit": git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip(),
 1685:             "files": files,
 1686:             "note": "Read-only context; omitted files were not included or exceeded the context limit.",
 1687:         }
 1688:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"candidate_source_context_{sequence}.json", context)
 1689:         return context
 1690:

 1740:         )

 1742:             run_dir=run_dir,

 1748:             timeout=config.role_timeout,
 1749:             reasoning=config.reviewer_reasoning,
 1750:         )

 1755:     def _create_checkpoint(self, worktree: Path, run_dir: Path, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
 1756:         """Capture a safe checkpoint of the worktree state with durable refs and binary fidelity."""
 1757:         # 1. Capture stash commit if any changes exist
 1758:         stash_res = command(["git", "stash", "create"], cwd=worktree)
 1759:         if stash_res.returncode != 0:
 1760:             raise FactoryError(f"git stash create failed: {stash_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1761:         stash_commit = stash_res.stdout.strip()
 1762:         stash_ref = None
 1763:         if stash_commit:
 1764:             stash_ref = f"refs/factory/checkpoints/{label}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
 1765:             ref_res = command(["git", "update-ref", stash_ref, stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1766:             if ref_res.returncode != 0:
 1767:                 raise FactoryError(f"git update-ref failed: {ref_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1768: 
 1769:         # 2. Capture diffs with returncode validation
 1770:         unstaged_res = command(["git", "diff"], cwd=worktree)
 1771:         if unstaged_res.returncode != 0:
 1772:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff failed: {unstaged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1773:         staged_res = command(["git", "diff", "--cached"], cwd=worktree)
 1774:         if staged_res.returncode != 0:
 1775:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff --cached failed: {staged_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1776: 
 1777:         # 3. Capture untracked files safely with NUL delimiter and base64 encoding
 1778:         untracked_res = command(["git", "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--exclude-standard"], cwd=worktree)
 1779:         if untracked_res.returncode != 0:
 1780:             raise FactoryError(f"git ls-files failed: {untracked_res.stderr}", "checkpoint")
 1781: 
 1782:         raw_paths = untracked_res.stdout.split("\0")
 1783:         untracked_files: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
 1784:         for rel_p in raw_paths:
 1785:             if not rel_p:
 1786:                 continue
 1787:             full_p = worktree / rel_p
 1788:             if full_p.is_symlink():
 1789:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1790:                     "type": "symlink",
 1791:                     "target": os.readlink(full_p),
 1792:                 }
 1793:             elif full_p.is_file():
 1794:                 content_bytes = full_p.read_bytes()
 1795:                 mode = full_p.stat().st_mode
 1796:                 untracked_files[rel_p] = {
 1797:                     "type": "file",
 1798:                     "data": base64.b64encode(content_bytes).decode("ascii"),
 1799:                     "mode": oct(mode),
 1800:                 }
 1801: 
 1802:         checkpoint = {
 1803:             "label": label,
 1804:             "created_at": now_iso(),
 1805:             "stash_commit": stash_commit,
 1806:             "stash_ref": stash_ref,
 1807:             "unstaged_diff": unstaged_res.stdout,
 1808:             "staged_diff": staged_res.stdout,
 1809:             "untracked_files": untracked_files,
 1810:         }
 1811:         atomic_json(run_dir / f"checkpoint_{label}.json", checkpoint)
 1812:         return checkpoint
 1813: 
 1814:     def _safe_rollback(self, worktree: Path, checkpoint: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path) -> None:
 1815:         """Safely restore the worktree to the state recorded in checkpoint and verify exact restoration."""
 1816:         # 1. Reset and clean strictly
 1817:         reset_res = command(["git", "reset", "--hard", "HEAD"], cwd=worktree)
 1818:         if reset_res.returncode != 0:
 1819:             raise FactoryError(f"git reset --hard failed: {reset_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1820:         clean_res = command(["git", "clean", "-fd", "-q"], cwd=worktree)
 1821:         if clean_res.returncode != 0:
 1822:             raise FactoryError(f"git clean failed: {clean_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1823: 
 1824:         # 2. Restore tracked changes
 1825:         stash_commit = checkpoint.get("stash_commit")
 1826:         if stash_commit:
 1827:             apply_res = command(["git", "stash", "apply", "--index", "--quiet", stash_commit], cwd=worktree)
 1828:             if apply_res.returncode != 0:
 1829:                 raise FactoryError(f"git stash apply --index failed: {apply_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1830:         else:
 1831:             staged_diff = checkpoint.get("staged_diff") or ""
 1832:             if staged_diff:
 1833:                 apply_staged = command(["git", "apply", "--index", "--whitespace=nowarn"], cwd=worktree, stdin=staged_diff)
 1834:                 if apply_staged.returncode != 0:
 1835:                     raise FactoryError(f"git apply --index failed: {apply_staged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1836:             unstaged_diff = checkpoint.get("unstaged_diff") or ""
 1837:             if unstaged_diff:
 1838:                 apply_unstaged = command(["git", "apply", "--whitespace=nowarn"], cwd=worktree, stdin=unstaged_diff)
 1839:                 if apply_unstaged.returncode != 0:
 1840:                     raise FactoryError(f"git apply unstaged failed: {apply_unstaged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1841: 
 1842:         # 3. Restore untracked files byte-for-byte and symlinks
 1843:         untracked_files = checkpoint.get("untracked_files") or {}
 1844:         for rel_p, file_meta in untracked_files.items():
 1845:             target_path = worktree / rel_p
 1846:             target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 1847:             if file_meta.get("type") == "symlink":
 1848:                 target = file_meta.get("target")
 1849:                 if target:
 1850:                     if target_path.is_symlink() or target_path.exists():
 1851:                         target_path.unlink()
 1852:                     os.symlink(target, target_path)
 1853:             elif file_meta.get("type") == "file":
 1854:                 data_b64 = file_meta.get("data", "")
 1855:                 content_bytes = base64.b64decode(data_b64)
 1856:                 target_path.write_bytes(content_bytes)
 1857:                 mode_str = file_meta.get("mode")
 1858:                 if mode_str and os.name != "nt":
 1859:                     try:
 1860:                         os.chmod(target_path, int(mode_str, 8))
 1861:                     except Exception as e:
 1862:                         raise FactoryError(f"Failed to restore mode for {rel_p}: {e}", "rollback")
 1863: 
 1864:         # 4. Post-rollback verification: confirm worktree state matches checkpoint exactly
 1865:         post_unstaged = command(["git", "diff"], cwd=worktree)
 1866:         if post_unstaged.returncode != 0:
 1867:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff verification failed: {post_unstaged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1868:         post_staged = command(["git", "diff", "--cached"], cwd=worktree)
 1869:         if post_staged.returncode != 0:
 1870:             raise FactoryError(f"git diff --cached verification failed: {post_staged.stderr}", "rollback")
 1871:         if post_unstaged.stdout != checkpoint.get("unstaged_diff", ""):
 1872:             raise FactoryError("rollback verification failed: unstaged diff mismatch", "rollback")
 1873:         if post_staged.stdout != checkpoint.get("staged_diff", ""):
 1874:             raise FactoryError("rollback verification failed: staged diff mismatch", "rollback")
 1875: 
 1876:         # 5. Post-rollback verification for untracked files: exact path set and content match
 1877:         post_untracked_res = command(["git", "ls-files", "-z", "--others", "--exclude-standard"], cwd=worktree)
 1878:         if post_untracked_res.returncode != 0:
 1879:             raise FactoryError(f"git ls-files verification failed: {post_untracked_res.stderr}", "rollback")
 1880:         post_raw_paths = [p for p in post_untracked_res.stdout.split("\0") if p]
 1881:         checkpoint_untracked = checkpoint.get("untracked_files") or {}
 1882:         if set(post_raw_paths) != set(checkpoint_untracked.keys()):
 1883:             raise FactoryError(
 1884:                 f"rollback verification failed: untracked path mismatch (expected {sorted(checkpoint_untracked.keys())}, got {sorted(post_raw_paths)})",
 1885:                 "rollback",
 1886:             )
 1887:         for rel_p in post_raw_paths:
 1888:             expected_meta = checkpoint_untracked[rel_p]
 1889:             current_p = worktree / rel_p
 1890:             if expected_meta.get("type") == "symlink":
 1891:                 if not current_p.is_symlink() or os.readlink(current_p) != expected_meta.get("target"):
 1892:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: symlink mismatch for {rel_p}", "rollback")
 1893:             elif expected_meta.get("type") == "file":
 1894:                 if not current_p.is_file():
 1895:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: {rel_p} is not a regular file", "rollback")
 1896:                 curr_bytes = current_p.read_bytes()
 1897:                 curr_b64 = base64.b64encode(curr_bytes).decode("ascii")
 1898:                 if curr_b64 != expected_meta.get("data"):
 1899:                     raise FactoryError(f"rollback verification failed: content mismatch for {rel_p}", "rollback")
 1900: 
 1901:         atomic_json(
 1902:             run_dir / "last_rollback.json",
 1903:             {"restored_to": checkpoint.get("label"), "timestamp": now_iso()},
 1904:         )
 1905:

  342: 
  343:     try:
  344:         prior_state = read_json(state_file)
  345:     except Exception as exc:

  376:     raw_issues = prior_ledger.get("issues", [])
  377:     if not isinstance(raw_issues, list):
  378:         raw_issues = []
  379: 
  380:     def issue_priority(item: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, str]:
  381:         st = item.get("status", "")
  382:         if st in {"open", "regressed"}:
  383:             return (0, str(item.get("id", "")))
  384:         return (1, str(item.get("id", "")))
  385: 
  386:     sorted_issues = sorted(raw_issues, key=issue_priority)
  387:     selected_issues: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  388:     current_chars = 0
  389:     truncated = False
  390:     omitted_count = 0
  391: 
  392:     for iss in sorted_issues:
  393:         if len(selected_issues) >= max_issues:
  394:             truncated = True
  395:             omitted_count = len(sorted_issues) - len(selected_issues)
  396:             break
  397: 
  398:         compact_iss = {
  399:             "id": iss.get("id"),
  400:             "status": iss.get("status"),
  401:             "source": iss.get("source"),
  402:             "title": (iss.get("title") or "")[:150],
  403:             "severity": iss.get("severity"),
  404:             "occurrences": iss.get("occurrences", 1),
  405:         }
  406:         iss_json_len = len(json.dumps(compact_iss))
  407:         if current_chars + iss_json_len > max_total_chars:
  408:             truncated = True
  409:             omitted_count = len(sorted_issues) - len(selected_issues)
  410:             break
  411:         selected_issues.append(compact_iss)
  412:         current_chars += iss_json_len
  413: 
  414:     context = {

  416:         "prior_phase": prior_state.get("phase"),

  526: def normalize_path(raw: str) -> str:
  527:     if not isinstance(raw, str):
  528:         raise FactoryError("file paths must be strings", "contract")
  529:     value = raw.strip().replace("\\", "/")
  530:     path = PurePosixPath(value)
  531:     if not value or path.is_absolute() or ".." in path.parts or path.parts[0] == ".git":
  532:         raise FactoryError(f"unsafe repository-relative path: {raw!r}", "contract")
  533:     normalized = path.as_posix()
  534:     if normalized in {".", ".git"}:
  535:         raise FactoryError(f"unsafe repository-relative path: {raw!r}", "contract")
  536:     return normalized
  537: 
  538: 
  539: def normalized_paths(values: Iterable[str]) -> list[str]:
  540:     result: list[str] = []
  541:     seen: set[str] = set()
  542:     for value in values:
  543:         path = normalize_path(value)
  544:         if path not in seen:
  545:             result.append(path)
  546:             seen.add(path)
  547:     if not result:
  548:         raise FactoryError("at least one allowed file is required", "contract")
  549:     return result
  550: 
  551: 
  552: def ensure_paths_confined(repo: Path, paths: Iterable[str]) -> None:
  553:     """Reject directories and symlink traversal outside the repository."""
  554:     root = repo.resolve()
  555:     for relative in paths:
  556:         candidate = repo / relative
  557:         resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=False)
  558:         try:
  559:             resolved.relative_to(root)
  560:         except ValueError as exc:
  561:             raise FactoryError(f"allowed path escapes repository through a symlink: {relative}", "scope") from exc
  562:         if candidate.is_symlink():
  563:             raise FactoryError(f"allowed path may not be a symlink: {relative}", "scope")
  564:         if candidate.exists() and candidate.is_dir():
  565:             raise FactoryError(f"allowed path is a directory, not a file: {relative}", "scope")
  566: 
  567: 
  568: def ensure_head_paths_not_symlinks(repo: Path, paths: Iterable[str]) -> None:
  569:     """Reject files that are symlinks in the committed source snapshot."""
  570:     for relative in paths:
  571:         entry = git(repo, "ls-tree", "HEAD", "--", relative).stdout.strip()
  572:         if entry and entry.split(maxsplit=1)[0] == "120000":
  573:             raise FactoryError(f"allowed path is a symlink in HEAD: {relative}", "scope")
  574: 
  575: 
  576: def parse_json_object(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  577:     candidate = text.strip()
  578:     # Match the complete fence contents. A non-greedy brace match truncates
  579:     # valid nested objects such as Sol review criteria at their first `}`.
  580:     fenced = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(.*?)\s*```", candidate, re.S | re.I)
  581:     if fenced:
  582:         candidate = fenced.group(1).strip()
  583:     else:
  584:         first, last = candidate.find("{"), candidate.rfind("}")
  585:         if first >= 0 and last > first:
  586:             candidate = candidate[first : last + 1]
  587:     try:
  588:         value = json.loads(candidate)
  589:     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
  590:         raise FactoryError(f"model did not return valid JSON: {exc}", "contract") from exc
  591:     if not isinstance(value, dict):
  592:         raise FactoryError("model response must be a JSON object", "contract")
  593:     return value

54:## Agy Builder & Cleanup rules
56:Treat Builder sandbox containment and execution parsing as fail-closed quality gates.
58:- For headless `agy CLI` execution, pass: `--sandbox`, `--model <model>`, `--output-format json`, `--disable-slash-commands`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, `--add-dir <worktree>`, and redirect `stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL`.
60:- Robust JSON response handling: `json.loads(stdout)` must explicitly verify `isinstance(parsed, dict)`. Agy CLI may output valid non-dict JSON (lists, primitives, null) or empty strings; fail closed with `FactoryError(..., "builder")` rather than allowing unhandled `AttributeError` on `.get("status")`.
61:- Fail-closed artifact cleanup: never recursively delete entire untracked directories (`.gemini`, `.agy`) before diff/status inspection, as this can conceal out-of-scope builder writes or erase pre-existing untracked user files.
63:- Only remove exact known builder-generated artifacts (e.g. `.aider.tags.cache.v4`, `.gemini/cache.db`, `.agy/cache`) after diff recording, and abort if untracked files cannot be confirmed clean.
75:- Deduplicate and bound slice target files before dispatching to Builder — passing the entire plan's `allowed_files` to every slice instruction causes unnecessary repository-map loading, context bloat, and prompt truncation in Aider.
76:- Keep exactly one implementation step for risky Builder-contract or launcher repairs — Aider can produce incomplete diffs when asked to modify large core files and broad test suites together.
77:- Feed every plan step to Aider separately — full requests and complete acceptance lists inflate input context and cause response-length failures.
78:- Preserve Luna in both repair routes — removing it because Aider is unreliable destroys the intended root-cause layer rather than fixing Builder reliability.
81:- In `clean_builder_generated_artifacts`, always purge builder runtime cache directories (`.gemini`, `.agy`, `.aider.tags.cache.v4`) from BOTH the active worktree and the source repo before computing Git status (`_source_status`, `_changed_files`) — otherwise untracked cache files created during builder execution cause false isolation or scope-violation exceptions.
82:- For `agy CLI` headless automation, pass `--output-format json`, `--disable-slash-commands`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`, and `--add-dir <worktree>` to avoid hanging on permission/sandbox prompts or generating unapplied scratch files outside the worktree.
83:- If Builder models accept bare model identifiers (e.g. `gemini-3.8-flash-high`), strip legacy provider prefixes (such as `openai/`) in `Config.__post_init__` to preserve compatibility with legacy presets while avoiding upstream invalid-model errors.
85:- Keep production diffs narrowly scoped — Aider can satisfy a local task while accidentally altering test-runner or orchestration behavior; reject those edits rather than merging and repairing later.
86:- Do not silently substitute Astra, Sol, Luna, or Builder models after a provider failure — role/model mismatch invalidates the pipeline's independent checks.
